== December 2017 - Repeat Login Campaign No. 54 ==
{| class="wikitable mw-collapsible mw-collapsed" data-expandtext="{{int:show}}" data-collapsetext="{{int:hide}}" style="min-width:800px"
! Rewards
|-
| 
[[File:15968.jpg|link=]]

This month, {{color|red|when players log in for the first time, they'll be rewarded with login points equivalent to the first three days' worth of points!}} Additionally, players will be able to purchase certain items at bargain basement prices—the perfect chance for those just coming back and seasoned veterans alike to earn a wide variety of rewards, with an alter ego and mount aimed especially at returnees!
